  <!-- ==============================================================
       INLINE CRITICAL JS — spinner wait + 8-second fallback
       ============================================================== -->
  <script>
    (function() {
      var ready = false;
      var TIMEOUT = 8000;
      var main = document.getElementById('obMain');

      function reveal() {
        if (ready) return;
        ready = true;
        if (main) {
          main.classList.add('ob-visible');
        }
        // Also initialise right-panel visibility
        var right = document.getElementById('obRight');
        if (right) right.classList.add('ob-visible');
        // Trigger the heavy spinner hide — include spinner.js handles it
        {% include "reusables/spinner.js" %}
      }

      var css = document.getElementById('heavy-css');
      if (css) {
        if (css.rel === 'stylesheet') { reveal(); }
        else { css.addEventListener('load', reveal); }
      }
      setTimeout(reveal, TIMEOUT);
    })();
  </script>

  <!-- ==============================================================
       FULL APP SCRIPT — phase engine, physics, particles
       Placed at bottom of body — no defer needed
       ============================================================== -->
  <script>
  (function() {
    'use strict';

    /* ============================================================
       SPRING PHYSICS ENGINE
       GTA-level: mass, damping, overshoot, settle
       ============================================================ */
    function Spring(cfg) {
      cfg = cfg || {};
      this.k  = cfg.stiffness || 170;   // spring constant
      this.c  = cfg.damping   || 26;    // damping coefficient
      this.m  = cfg.mass      || 1;     // mass
      this.x  = 0;                      // position
      this.v  = 0;                      // velocity
      this.t  = 0;                      // target
      this.lt = null;                   // last time
    }
    Spring.prototype.to = function(target) { this.t = target; return this; };
    Spring.prototype.step = function(now) {
      if (!this.lt) { this.lt = now; return this.x; }
      var dt = Math.min((now - this.lt) / 1000, 0.064);
      this.lt = now;
      var f = -this.k * (this.x - this.t) - this.c * this.v;
      this.v += (f / this.m) * dt;
      this.x += this.v * dt;
      return this.x;
    };
    Spring.prototype.settled = function() {
      return Math.abs(this.v) < 0.008 && Math.abs(this.x - this.t) < 0.008;
    };

    /* ============================================================
       CANVAS PARTICLE SYSTEM — desktop only
       Particles drift, connect to neighbours, react to mouse
       ============================================================ */
    var canvas  = document.getElementById('ob-canvas');
    var ctx     = canvas && canvas.getContext('2d');
    var mouse   = { x: -9999, y: -9999 };
    var particles = [];
    var W, H;

    function resizeCanvas() {
      if (!canvas) return;
      W = canvas.width  = window.innerWidth;
      H = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
    }

    function initParticles() {
      particles = [];
      var count = Math.min(Math.floor(W / 18), 70);
      for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        particles.push({
          x:  Math.random() * W,
          y:  Math.random() * H,
          vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25,
          vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25,
          r:  Math.random() * 1.4 + 0.4,
          a:  Math.random() * 0.4 + 0.08
        });
      }
    }

    function drawParticles() {
      if (!ctx || window.innerWidth < 900) return;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
      for (var i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
        var p = particles[i];
        // Mouse attraction
        var dx = mouse.x - p.x, dy = mouse.y - p.y;
        var dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
        if (dist < 180 && dist > 0) {
          p.vx += dx / dist * 0.018;
          p.vy += dy / dist * 0.018;
        }
        // Friction
        p.vx *= 0.985;
        p.vy *= 0.985;
        // Wrap
        p.x = ((p.x + p.vx) + W) % W;
        p.y = ((p.y + p.vy) + H) % H;
        // Draw
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(59,130,246,' + p.a + ')';
        ctx.fill();
        // Connections
        for (var j = i + 1; j < particles.length; j++) {
          var q = particles[j];
          var ddx = p.x - q.x, ddy = p.y - q.y;
          var d = Math.sqrt(ddx*ddx + ddy*ddy);
          if (d < 110) {
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
            ctx.lineTo(q.x, q.y);
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(59,130,246,' + ((1 - d/110) * 0.12) + ')';
            ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(drawParticles);
    }

    if (canvas) {
      resizeCanvas();
      initParticles();
      requestAnimationFrame(drawParticles);
      window.addEventListener('resize', function() { resizeCanvas(); initParticles(); });
      window.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) { mouse.x = e.clientX; mouse.y = e.clientY; });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       PARTICLE BURST — fires on CTA clicks
       ============================================================ */
    function burst(el, count) {
      count = count || 16;
      var rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      var cx = rect.left + rect.width  / 2;
      var cy = rect.top  + rect.height / 2;
      var colors = ['#60a5fa','#818cf8','#a78bfa','#3b82f6','#93c5fd'];
      for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        (function(i) {
          var dot = document.createElement('div');
          var angle = (i / count) * Math.PI * 2;
          var speed = 55 + Math.random() * 90;
          var tx = Math.cos(angle) * speed;
          var ty = Math.sin(angle) * speed;
          var size = 4 + Math.random() * 4;
          dot.style.cssText = [
            'position:fixed',
            'border-radius:50%',
            'pointer-events:none',
            'z-index:9999',
            'width:'  + size + 'px',
            'height:' + size + 'px',
            'left:'   + (cx - size/2) + 'px',
            'top:'    + (cy - size/2) + 'px',
            'background:' + colors[i % colors.length]
          ].join(';');
          document.body.appendChild(dot);
          if (dot.animate) {
            dot.animate([
              { transform:'translate(0,0)',                       opacity:1 },
              { transform:'translate('+tx+'px,'+ty+'px) scale(0)', opacity:0 }
            ], {
              duration: 450 + Math.random() * 250,
              easing: 'cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)',
              fill: 'forwards'
            }).onfinish = function() { dot.remove(); };
          } else {
            setTimeout(function(){ dot.remove(); }, 700);
          }
        })(i);
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       CARD 3D TILT — desktop mouse tracking
       ============================================================ */
    function initTilt(card) {
      if (!card || window.innerWidth < 900) return;
      card.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
        var r = card.getBoundingClientRect();
        var nx = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width  - 0.5; // -0.5..0.5
        var ny = (e.clientY - r.top)  / r.height - 0.5;
        card.style.transform = 'perspective(800px) rotateY(' + (nx*6) + 'deg) rotateX(' + (-ny*4) + 'deg)';
      });
      card.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
        card.style.transform = 'perspective(800px) rotateY(0deg) rotateX(0deg)';
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       STATE
       ============================================================ */
    var STATE = {
      phase: 1,
      name: '',
      what: '',
      category: '',
      why: '',
      minimum: '',
      goalDays: 365,
      goalForever: false,
      checkinTime: '21:00',
      reminderOn: true,
      reminderTime: '20:30',
      reminderMethod: 'email',
      waNumber: '',
      socialMode: 'solo',
      friendUUID: '',
      friendName: '',
      leaderboard: true,
      theme: 'dark',
      aiInsights: true,
      weeklyReport: true,
      newsletter: false,
      STORAGE_KEY: 'sd_ob_v2'
    };

    /* ============================================================
       PERSISTENCE
       ============================================================ */
    function save() {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STATE.STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
          phase: STATE.phase,
          name: STATE.name,
          what: STATE.what,
          category: STATE.category,
          why: STATE.why,
          minimum: STATE.minimum,
          goalDays: STATE.goalDays,
          goalForever: STATE.goalForever,
          checkinTime: STATE.checkinTime,
          reminderOn: STATE.reminderOn,
          reminderTime: STATE.reminderTime,
          reminderMethod: STATE.reminderMethod,
          waNumber: STATE.waNumber,
          socialMode: STATE.socialMode,
          friendUUID: STATE.friendUUID,
          friendName: STATE.friendName,
          leaderboard: STATE.leaderboard,
          theme: STATE.theme,
          aiInsights: STATE.aiInsights,
          weeklyReport: STATE.weeklyReport,
          newsletter: STATE.newsletter
        }));
      } catch(e) {}
    }

    function loadSaved() {
      try {
        var raw = localStorage.getItem(STATE.STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!raw) return false;
        var d = JSON.parse(raw);
        Object.assign(STATE, d);
        return true;
      } catch(e) { return false; }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       POPULATE CALENDARS (right panel + preview)
       ============================================================ */
    function buildCal(container) {
      if (!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = '';
      var today = new Date().getDate() - 1; // 0-indexed day of month
      var days = new Date(new Date().getFullYear(), new Date().getMonth()+1, 0).getDate();
      var show = Math.min(days, 35);
      for (var i = 0; i < show; i++) {
        var cell = document.createElement('div');
        cell.className = 'dw-cell' + (i === today ? ' today' : '');
        container.appendChild(cell);
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       LIVE DASHBOARD PREVIEW UPDATES
       ============================================================ */
    function updateDash() {
      // Greeting name
      var dwName = document.getElementById('dwName');
      if (dwName) {
        var n = STATE.name || 'Friend';
        if (dwName.textContent !== n) {
          dwName.textContent = n;
          dwName.classList.remove('dw-pop');
          void dwName.offsetWidth; // reflow
          dwName.classList.add('dw-pop');
        }
      }
      // Commitment
      var dwCommitTxt = document.getElementById('dwCommitTxt');
      if (dwCommitTxt) {
        if (STATE.what) {
          if (dwCommitTxt.textContent !== STATE.what) {
            dwCommitTxt.textContent = STATE.what;
            dwCommitTxt.classList.remove('ph','dw-pop');
            void dwCommitTxt.offsetWidth;
            dwCommitTxt.classList.add('dw-pop');
            var dc = document.getElementById('dwCommit');
            if (dc) { dc.classList.remove('dw-updated'); void dc.offsetWidth; dc.classList.add('dw-updated'); }
          }
        } else {
          dwCommitTxt.textContent = 'Will appear as you type...';
          dwCommitTxt.classList.add('ph');
        }
      }
      // Why
      var dwWhy = document.getElementById('dwWhy');
      if (dwWhy) {
        if (STATE.why) {
          dwWhy.textContent = '"' + STATE.why.slice(0,80) + (STATE.why.length > 80 ? '…' : '') + '"';
          dwWhy.classList.remove('ph');
        } else {
          dwWhy.textContent = 'Your "why" will appear in Step 3...';
          dwWhy.classList.add('ph');
        }
      }
      // Reminder
      var dwRem = document.getElementById('dwReminder');
      if (dwRem) {
        var m = STATE.reminderMethod.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + STATE.reminderMethod.slice(1);
        dwRem.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-bell"></i> <span>Reminder at '+ STATE.reminderTime +' via '+ m +'</span>';
      }
      // Also update phase-11 preview
      var mn = document.getElementById('mockName');
      if (mn) mn.textContent = STATE.name || '—';
      var mc = document.getElementById('mockCommit');
      if (mc) mc.textContent = STATE.what || '—';
      var mw = document.getElementById('mockWhy');
      if (mw) mw.textContent = STATE.why ? '"' + STATE.why.slice(0,100) + '"' : 'Your "why" will appear here.';
      var mr = document.getElementById('mockReminder');
      if (mr) {
        var mm = STATE.reminderMethod.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + STATE.reminderMethod.slice(1);
        mr.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-bell" style="color:var(--accL)"></i><span>Reminder at '+STATE.reminderTime+' via '+mm+'</span>';
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       PROGRESS
       ============================================================ */
    function updateProgress(n) {
      var fill = document.getElementById('obFill');
      if (fill) fill.style.width = ((n / 12) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%';
      var lbl = document.getElementById('obPhaseLbl');
      var labels = [
        '', 'Welcome {{request.user.username}}','About S&D','Your Name','Your Vow','Your Why',
        'Minimum','Your Goal','Schedule','Accountability',
        'Preferences','Preview','Launch 🚀'
      ];
      if (lbl) lbl.textContent = labels[n] || '';
      // Dots
      var dots = document.querySelectorAll('.ob-dot');
      dots.forEach(function(d, i) {
        var phNum = parseInt(d.dataset.ph);
        d.classList.remove('active','done');
        if (phNum === n) d.classList.add('active');
        else if (phNum < n) d.classList.add('done');
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       SPLIT MODE — show/hide right dashboard panel
       ============================================================ */
    var splitPhases = {3:1,4:1,5:1,6:1,7:1,8:1,9:1,10:1};
    function updateSplit(n) {
      var wrap  = document.getElementById('obWrap');
      var right = document.getElementById('obRight');
      if (!wrap || !right) return;
      if (splitPhases[n] && window.innerWidth >= 900) {
        wrap.classList.add('ob-split');
        right.classList.add('ob-visible');
      } else {
        wrap.classList.remove('ob-split');
        right.classList.remove('ob-visible');
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       PHASE NAVIGATION
       ============================================================ */
    var currentPhase = 1;

    function goTo(n) {
      if (n < 1 || n > 12) return;
      var old = document.getElementById('p' + currentPhase);
      var next = document.getElementById('p' + n);
      if (!next) return;
      currentPhase = n;
      STATE.phase = n;
      // Hide all, activate new
      document.querySelectorAll('.ob-phase').forEach(function(el) {
        el.classList.remove('ob-active');
      });
      next.classList.add('ob-active');
      // Init tilts on new card
      var card = next.querySelector('.ob-card');
      if (card) initTilt(card);
      updateProgress(n);
      updateSplit(n);
      updateDash();
      populateSummary();
      save();
      window.scrollTo({ top:0, behavior:'smooth' });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       VALIDATION
       ============================================================ */
    function validatePhase(n) {
      var ok = true;
      function fail(id, errId) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        var err = document.getElementById(errId);
        if (el && !el.value.trim()) {
          el.classList.add('ob-err');
          if (err) err.classList.add('show');
          if (ok) { // shake first fail
            el.style.animation = 'none';
            el.getBoundingClientRect();
            el.style.animation = 'shakeX 0.4s ease';
          }
          ok = false;
        }
      }
      if (n === 3) fail('obName','nameErr');
      if (n === 4) fail('obWhat','whatErr');
      if (n === 5) fail('obWhy','whyErr');
      if (n === 6) fail('obMin','minErr');
      return ok;
    }

    // Clear error on type
    ['obName','obWhat','obWhy','obMin'].forEach(function(id) {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener('input', function() {
        el.classList.remove('ob-err');
        var err = el.parentElement.querySelector('.ob-field-err');
        if (err) err.classList.remove('show');
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       COLLECT STATE FROM FORM
       ============================================================ */
    function collectState() {
      var n = document.getElementById('obName');   if(n) STATE.name = n.value.trim();
      var w = document.getElementById('obWhat');   if(w) STATE.what = w.value.trim();
      var y = document.getElementById('obWhy');    if(y) STATE.why  = y.value.trim();
      var m = document.getElementById('obMin');    if(m) STATE.minimum = m.value.trim();
      var ct = document.getElementById('obCheckinTime'); if(ct) STATE.checkinTime = ct.value;
      var ron = document.getElementById('obReminderOn'); if(ron) STATE.reminderOn = ron.checked;
      var rt = document.getElementById('obReminderTime'); if(rt) STATE.reminderTime = rt.value;
      var wa = document.getElementById('obWaNum'); if(wa) STATE.waNumber = wa.value.trim();
      var ldr = document.getElementById('obLeaderboard'); if(ldr) STATE.leaderboard = ldr.checked;
      var ai  = document.getElementById('obAI'); if(ai) STATE.aiInsights = ai.checked;
      var wr  = document.getElementById('obWeeklyReport'); if(wr) STATE.weeklyReport = wr.checked;
      var nl  = document.getElementById('obNewsletter'); if(nl) STATE.newsletter = nl.checked;
      var fw  = document.getElementById('obForever'); if(fw) STATE.goalForever = fw.checked;
    }

    /* ============================================================
       RESTORE FORM FROM STATE
       ============================================================ */
    function restoreForm() {
      function set(id, val) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.value = val || '';
      }
      set('obName', STATE.name);
      set('obWhat', STATE.what);
      set('obWhy',  STATE.why);
      set('obMin',  STATE.minimum);
      set('obCheckinTime', STATE.checkinTime);
      set('obReminderTime', STATE.reminderTime);
      set('obWaNum', STATE.waNumber);

      var ron = document.getElementById('obReminderOn');
      if(ron) ron.checked = STATE.reminderOn;
      var ldr = document.getElementById('obLeaderboard');
      if(ldr) ldr.checked = STATE.leaderboard;
      var ai = document.getElementById('obAI');
      if(ai) ai.checked = STATE.aiInsights;
      var wr = document.getElementById('obWeeklyReport');
      if(wr) wr.checked = STATE.weeklyReport;
      var nl = document.getElementById('obNewsletter');
      if(nl) nl.checked = STATE.newsletter;
      var fw = document.getElementById('obForever');
      if(fw) fw.checked = STATE.goalForever;

      // Category
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-cat]').forEach(function(c) {
        c.classList.toggle('on', c.dataset.cat === STATE.category);
      });
      // Goal
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-days]').forEach(function(g) {
        g.classList.toggle('on', parseInt(g.dataset.days) === STATE.goalDays);
      });
      // Method
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-method]').forEach(function(mm) {
        mm.classList.toggle('on', mm.dataset.method === STATE.reminderMethod);
      });
      // Social
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach(function(s) {
        s.classList.toggle('on', s.dataset.mode === STATE.socialMode);
      });
      // Theme
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(function(t) {
        t.classList.toggle('on', t.dataset.theme === STATE.theme);
      });
      // Theme on body
      if (STATE.theme === 'light') document.body.classList.add('sd-light');
      // WhatsApp field
      var waField = document.getElementById('ob-wa-field');
      if (waField) waField.classList.toggle('show', STATE.reminderMethod === 'whatsapp');
      // Friend section
      var fs = document.getElementById('friendSection');
      if (fs) fs.style.display = STATE.socialMode === 'friend' ? 'block' : 'none';
    }

    /* ============================================================
       POPULATE SUMMARY (phase 12)
       ============================================================ */
    function populateSummary() {
      collectState();
      var m = document.getElementById('sumWhat');   if(m) m.textContent = STATE.what || '—';
      var y = document.getElementById('sumWhy');    if(y) y.textContent = STATE.why  || '—';
      var mn = document.getElementById('sumMin');   if(mn) mn.textContent = STATE.minimum || '—';
      var g = document.getElementById('sumGoal');
      if(g) g.textContent = STATE.goalForever ? 'Lifelong' : (STATE.goalDays + ' days');
      var r = document.getElementById('sumReminder');
      if(r) r.textContent = STATE.reminderOn
        ? (STATE.reminderTime + ' via ' + STATE.reminderMethod)
        : 'Off';
      var md = document.getElementById('sumMode');
      if(md) md.textContent = STATE.socialMode === 'friend' && STATE.friendName
        ? 'With ' + STATE.friendName
        : STATE.socialMode === 'friend' ? 'With partner' : 'Solo';
    }

    /* ============================================================
       BUILD TIME SELECT OPTIONS
       ============================================================ */
    function buildTimeSelect(selId, def) {
      var sel = document.getElementById(selId);
      if (!sel) return;
      for (var h = 0; h < 24; h++) {
        for (var n = 0; n < 60; n += 30) {
          var t = (h < 10 ? '0' : '') + h + ':' + (n === 0 ? '00' : '30');
          var opt = document.createElement('option');
          opt.value = t; opt.textContent = t;
          if (t === def) opt.selected = true;
          sel.appendChild(opt);
        }
      }
    }
    buildTimeSelect('obCheckinTime',  '21:00');
    buildTimeSelect('obReminderTime', '20:30');

    /* ============================================================
       BUILD DOTS (mobile)
       ============================================================ */
    var dotsEl = document.getElementById('obDots');
    if (dotsEl) {
      for (var d = 1; d <= 12; d++) {
        var dot = document.createElement('button');
        dot.className = 'ob-dot';
        dot.dataset.ph = d;
        dot.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Phase ' + d);
        dotsEl.appendChild(dot);
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       BUILD CALENDARS
       ============================================================ */
    buildCal(document.getElementById('dwCal'));
    buildCal(document.getElementById('mockCal'));

    /* ============================================================
       BUTTON DELEGATION — data-next / data-prev
       ============================================================ */
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var btn = e.target.closest('[data-next]');
      if (btn && !btn.closest('.ob-right')) {
        var to = parseInt(btn.dataset.next);
        var reqId = btn.dataset.req;
        var errId = btn.dataset.err;
        if (reqId) {
          var el = document.getElementById(reqId);
          if (el && !el.value.trim()) {
            el.classList.add('ob-err');
            var err = document.getElementById(errId);
            if(err) err.classList.add('show');
            el.focus();
            return;
          }
        }
        if (!validatePhase(currentPhase)) return;
        collectState();
        burst(btn);
        goTo(to);
      }

      var prevBtn = e.target.closest('[data-prev]');
      if (prevBtn) {
        collectState();
        goTo(parseInt(prevBtn.dataset.prev));
      }
    });

    /* ============================================================
       LIVE INPUT UPDATES → dashboard preview
       ============================================================ */
    ['obName','obWhat','obWhy'].forEach(function(id) {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener('input', function() {
        collectState();
        updateDash();
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       CATEGORY CHIPS
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-cat]').forEach(function(c) {
      c.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-cat]').forEach(function(cc){cc.classList.remove('on')});
        c.classList.add('on');
        STATE.category = c.dataset.cat;
        save();
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       SUGGESTION CHIPS (fill input)
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-fill]').forEach(function(chip) {
      chip.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var target = document.getElementById(chip.dataset.fill);
        if (target) {
          target.value = chip.textContent;
          target.focus();
          chip.classList.add('on');
          setTimeout(function(){ chip.classList.remove('on'); }, 1000);
          collectState();
          updateDash();
        }
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       GOAL CARDS
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-days]').forEach(function(g) {
      g.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-days]').forEach(function(gg){gg.classList.remove('on')});
        g.classList.add('on');
        STATE.goalDays = parseInt(g.dataset.days);
        var fw = document.getElementById('obForever');
        if(fw) fw.checked = false;
        STATE.goalForever = false;
        save();
      });
    });

    var foreverToggle = document.getElementById('obForever');
    if (foreverToggle) {
      foreverToggle.addEventListener('change', function() {
        if (this.checked) {
          document.querySelectorAll('[data-days]').forEach(function(g){g.classList.remove('on')});
          STATE.goalForever = true;
        }
        save();
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       METHOD CARDS (reminder channel)
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-method]').forEach(function(mc) {
      mc.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-method]').forEach(function(mm){mm.classList.remove('on')});
        mc.classList.add('on');
        STATE.reminderMethod = mc.dataset.method;
        var waField = document.getElementById('ob-wa-field');
        if (waField) waField.classList.toggle('show', STATE.reminderMethod === 'whatsapp');
        updateDash();
        save();
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       REMINDER TOGGLE
       ============================================================ */
    var remOn = document.getElementById('obReminderOn');
    var remSec = document.getElementById('obReminderSection');
    if (remOn && remSec) {
      remOn.addEventListener('change', function() {
        remSec.style.display = this.checked ? '' : 'none';
        STATE.reminderOn = this.checked;
        save();
      });
    }

    document.getElementById('obReminderTime') && document.getElementById('obReminderTime').addEventListener('change', function() {
      STATE.reminderTime = this.value;
      updateDash();
      save();
    });
    document.getElementById('obCheckinTime') && document.getElementById('obCheckinTime').addEventListener('change', function() {
      STATE.checkinTime = this.value;
      save();
    });

    /* ============================================================
       SOCIAL MODE CARDS
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach(function(sc) {
      sc.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach(function(s){s.classList.remove('on')});
        sc.classList.add('on');
        STATE.socialMode = sc.dataset.mode;
        var fs = document.getElementById('friendSection');
        if(fs) fs.style.display = STATE.socialMode === 'friend' ? 'block' : 'none';
        save();
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       THEME PICKER
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(function(t) {
      t.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(function(tt){tt.classList.remove('on')});
        t.classList.add('on');
        STATE.theme = t.dataset.theme;
        if (STATE.theme === 'light') {
          document.body.classList.add('sd-light');
          setCookie('sd-theme','light',365);
        } else {
          document.body.classList.remove('sd-light');
          setCookie('sd-theme','dark',365);
        }
        var icon = document.getElementById('obThemeIcon');
        if (icon) icon.className = STATE.theme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-sun' : 'fas fa-moon';
        save();
      });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       HEADER THEME BUTTON
       ============================================================ */
    var themeBtn = document.getElementById('obThemeBtn');
    if (themeBtn) {
      themeBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var isDark = !document.body.classList.contains('sd-light');
        var toTheme = isDark ? 'light' : 'dark';
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(function(t) {
          t.classList.toggle('on', t.dataset.theme === toTheme);
        });
        STATE.theme = toTheme;
        document.body.classList.toggle('sd-light', toTheme === 'light');
        setCookie('sd-theme', toTheme, 365);
        var icon = document.getElementById('obThemeIcon');
        if (icon) icon.className = toTheme === 'light' ? 'fas fa-sun' : 'fas fa-moon';
        save();
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       FRIEND SEARCH
       ============================================================ */
    function friendState(which) {
      ['frLoading','frFound','frSent','frNone','frError','frSkipped'].forEach(function(id) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        if(el) el.style.display = 'none';
      });
      var show = document.getElementById(which);
      if(show) show.style.display = which === 'frFound' ? 'flex' : 'block';
    }

    var searchBtn = document.getElementById('searchFriendBtn');
    if (searchBtn) {
      searchBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var code = document.getElementById('obFriendCode').value.trim();
        if (!code) return;
        friendState('frLoading');
        searchBtn.disabled = true;
        var fd = new FormData();
        fd.append('uuid', code);
        fetch('{% url "origin_search_friend" %}', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCookie('csrftoken') },
          body: fd,
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000)
        }).then(function(r) {
          return r.json().then(function(d) { return { status: r.status, data: d }; });
        }).then(function(res) {
          searchBtn.disabled = false;
          if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 400) {
            document.getElementById('frName').textContent = res.data.name;
            document.getElementById('frId').textContent   = '@' + (res.data.username || res.data.userid);
            document.getElementById('frAvatar').src       = res.data.profile_image || '';
            STATE.friendName = res.data.name;
            STATE.friendUUID = code;
            friendState('frFound');
            save();
          } else if (res.status === 404) {
            friendState('frNone');
          } else {
            document.getElementById('frErrMsg').textContent = res.data.message || 'Something went wrong.';
            friendState('frError');
          }
        }).catch(function() {
          searchBtn.disabled = false;
          document.getElementById('frErrMsg').textContent = 'Request timed out. Try again.';
          friendState('frError');
        });
      });
    }

    var addFriendBtn = document.getElementById('addFriendBtn');
    if (addFriendBtn) {
      addFriendBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var uid = document.getElementById('frId').textContent.replace('@','');
        var fd = new FormData(); fd.append('userid', uid);
        fetch('{% url "origin_add_friend" %}', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'X-CSRFToken': getCookie('csrftoken') },
          body: fd
        }).then(function(r) {
          return r.json().then(function(d) { return { status: r.status, data: d }; });
        }).then(function(res) {
          if (res.status === 200) { friendState('frSent'); save(); }
          else {
            document.getElementById('frErrMsg').textContent = res.data.message || 'Could not send request.';
            friendState('frError');
          }
        }).catch(function() {
          document.getElementById('frErrMsg').textContent = 'Something went wrong.';
          friendState('frError');
        });
      });
    }

    var skipFriendBtn = document.getElementById('skipFriendBtn');
    if (skipFriendBtn) {
      skipFriendBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        STATE.friendUUID = ''; STATE.friendName = '';
        friendState('frSkipped');
        save();
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       SUBMIT — POST JSON to /dummy_endpoint
       ============================================================ */
    var submitBtn = document.getElementById('submitBtn');
    if (submitBtn) {
      submitBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        collectState();
        submitBtn.classList.add('ob-loading');
        submitBtn.disabled = true;

        var payload = {
          meta: {
            completed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            timezone: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || '',
            user_agent: navigator.userAgent
          },
          user: {
            name: STATE.name
          },
          commitment: {
            what:     STATE.what,
            category: STATE.category,
            why:      STATE.why,
            minimum:  STATE.minimum,
            goal_days: STATE.goalForever ? 0 : STATE.goalDays
          },
          schedule: {
            checkin_time:     STATE.checkinTime,
            reminder_enabled: STATE.reminderOn,
            reminder_time:    STATE.reminderTime,
            reminder_method:  STATE.reminderMethod,
            whatsapp_number:  STATE.waNumber
          },
          social: {
            mode:            STATE.socialMode,
            friend_uuid:     STATE.friendUUID,
            friend_name:     STATE.friendName,
            leaderboard_optin: STATE.leaderboard
          },
          preferences: {
            theme:         STATE.theme,
            ai_insights:   STATE.aiInsights,
            weekly_report: STATE.weeklyReport,
            newsletter:    STATE.newsletter
          }
        };

        fetch('{% url "origin_database" %}', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'X-CSRFToken': getCookie('csrftoken')
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        }).then(function(r) {
          return r.json();
        }).then(function(d) {
          localStorage.removeItem(STATE.STORAGE_KEY);
          var dest = d.redirect || '{{ where_to_go_full_url }}?';
          console.log(payload)
          window.location.href = dest;
        }).catch(function() {
          submitBtn.classList.remove('ob-loading');
          submitBtn.disabled = false;
          // Even on network error, clear and redirect if we have a URL
          localStorage.removeItem(STATE.STORAGE_KEY);
          window.location.href = '{{ where_to_go_full_url }}';
        });
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       RESTART BUTTON
       ============================================================ */
    var restartBtn = document.getElementById('restartBtn');
    if (restartBtn) {
      restartBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (confirm('Clear all onboarding data and start over?')) {
          localStorage.removeItem(STATE.STORAGE_KEY);
          window.location.reload();
        }
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       TOUCH SWIPE (mobile) — velocity-aware, spring feel
       ============================================================ */
    var touchStart = { x:0, y:0, t:0 };
    var phases = document.getElementById('obPhases');
    if (phases) {
      phases.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
        touchStart.x = e.touches[0].clientX;
        touchStart.y = e.touches[0].clientY;
        touchStart.t = Date.now();
      }, { passive: true });

      phases.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
        var dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - touchStart.x;
        var dy = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - touchStart.y;
        var dt = Date.now() - touchStart.t;
        var vx = Math.abs(dx) / dt;

        // Only count horizontal swipes with enough velocity/distance
        if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) && Math.abs(dx) > 55 && vx > 0.28) {
          if (dx < 0 && currentPhase < 12) {
            collectState();
            if (validatePhase(currentPhase)) goTo(currentPhase + 1);
          } else if (dx > 0 && currentPhase > 1) {
            collectState();
            goTo(currentPhase - 1);
          }
        }
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       COOKIE HELPERS
       ============================================================ */
    function getCookie(name) {
      var v = document.cookie.match('(?:^|;) ?' + name + '=([^;]*)(;|$)');
      return v ? decodeURIComponent(v[1]) : '';
    }
    function setCookie(name, value, days) {
      var d = new Date();
      d.setDate(d.getDate() + (days || 1));
      document.cookie = name + '=' + encodeURIComponent(value) + ';path=/;expires=' + d.toUTCString();
    }

    /* ============================================================
       SHAKE KEYFRAME (inline for browsers that need it)
       ============================================================ */
    var shakeStyle = document.createElement('style');
    shakeStyle.textContent = '@keyframes shakeX{0%,100%{transform:translateX(0)}25%{transform:translateX(-5px)}75%{transform:translateX(5px)}}';
    document.head.appendChild(shakeStyle);

    /* ============================================================
       INIT — restore saved state and go to saved phase
       ============================================================ */
    var hasData = loadSaved();
    restoreForm();
    updateDash();
    updateProgress(STATE.phase);

    if (hasData && STATE.phase > 1) {
      goTo(STATE.phase);
    } else {
      goTo(1);
    }

    /* ============================================================
       AUTO-SAVE on any input change across the whole form
       ============================================================ */
    document.querySelectorAll('.ob-input,.ob-switch input').forEach(function(el) {
      el.addEventListener('change', function() { collectState(); updateDash(); save(); });
      el.addEventListener('input',  function() { collectState(); updateDash(); });
    });

    /* ============================================================
       THEME INIT from cookie
       ============================================================ */
    (function() {
      var saved = getCookie('sd-theme') || STATE.theme;
      if (saved === 'light') {
        document.body.classList.add('sd-light');
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme]').forEach(function(t) {
          t.classList.toggle('on', t.dataset.theme === 'light');
        });
        var icon = document.getElementById('obThemeIcon');
        if (icon) icon.className = 'fas fa-sun';
        STATE.theme = 'light';
      }
    })();

    /* ============================================================
       HEAVY CSS FALLBACK (set rel=stylesheet if not done yet)
       ============================================================ */
    var hcss = document.getElementById('heavy-css');
    if (hcss && hcss.rel !== 'stylesheet') hcss.rel = 'stylesheet';

  })();
  </script>

</body>
</html>

also there is the error/ folder inside the templates for rendering error respectively based on status code 

next is static/
this folder is segmented into cdn/ css/ abd img/

in the cdn/ 
demo_weekly_report.html

/* ============================================================
   cdn/demo_weekly_analysis.css
   ENGINE STYLESHEET — loaded async via <link rel="preload">,
   then promoted to a real stylesheet once downloaded, so it
   simply cascades OVER demo_weekly_analysis.css (same class
   names, later in source order = wins). This file owns every
   painted texture, 3D tilt, spring-physics entrance, and the
   hand-drawn discipline visuals. Nothing here should be load-
   bearing for legibility — if it fails to load, the light CSS
   already left the page usable.

   Motion model:
   - html[data-input="touch"]  → ambient physics animations run
     automatically (no hover exists on touch, so motion has to
     announce itself). Set by inline JS via a pointer/hover query.
   - html[data-input="fine"]   → components stay calm at rest and
     only escalate into full 3D on :hover.
   - html.reduced-motion       → every animation above is stripped.
   ============================================================ */

    .day-list .day-card:active{transform:translateX(0) rotate(0deg)!important}
}

landing_page.css (this is the larger slower to load css for the landing_page), i still have ...another landing_page.css in the css/ folder 
most file inside the cdn/ are heavy files that are only loaded after the light one in the css/ have loaded

landing_page.js
/**
 * ADVANCED JS â€” STREAK & DISCIPLINE
 * Loaded after page is interactive.
 * Features: theme toggle with cookie, mobile menu, scatter animations,
 *           scroll progress, intersection observer reveals, smooth scroll.
 */
(function() {
    'use strict';

    // ===== DOM ELEMENTS =====
    const body = document.body;
    const header = document.getElementById('siteHeader');
    const themeToggle = document.getElementById('themeToggle');
    const mobileToggle = document.getElementById('mobileToggle');
    const navLinks = document.getElementById('navLinks');
    const mobileOverlay = document.getElementById('mobileOverlay');
    const scatterElements = document.querySelectorAll('[data-scatter]');
    const lazySections = document.querySelectorAll('.lazy-section');
    const lazyImages = document.querySelectorAll('img.lazy-img');

    // ===== THEME TOGGLE WITH COOKIE =====
    const THEME_COOKIE = 'sd-theme';
    
    function getCookie(name) {
        const value = `; ${document.cookie}`;
        const parts = value.split(`; ${name}=`);
        if (parts.length === 2) return parts.pop().split(';').shift();
        return null;
    }
    
    function setCookie(name, value, days = 365) {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days * 86400000));
        document.cookie = `${name}=${value};expires=${d.toUTCString()};path=/;SameSite=Lax`;
    }

    // Apply saved theme immediately
    const savedTheme = getCookie(THEME_COOKIE);
    if (savedTheme === 'light') {
        body.classList.add('light-mode');
        themeToggle.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sun"></i>';
    } else {
        themeToggle.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-moon"></i>';
    }
    
    // Show toggle button now that JS is ready
    themeToggle.style.display = 'flex';
    
    themeToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (body.classList.contains('light-mode')) {
            body.classList.remove('light-mode');
            setCookie(THEME_COOKIE, 'dark');
            themeToggle.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-moon"></i>';
        } else {
            body.classList.add('light-mode');
            setCookie(THEME_COOKIE, 'light');
            themeToggle.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sun"></i>';
        }
    });

    // ===== MOBILE MENU =====
    function openMenu() {
        navLinks.classList.add('active');
        mobileOverlay.classList.add('active');
        mobileToggle.classList.add('active');
        body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }
    
    function closeMenu() {
        navLinks.classList.remove('active');
        mobileOverlay.classList.remove('active');
        mobileToggle.classList.remove('active');
        body.style.overflow = '';
    }
    
    mobileToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (navLinks.classList.contains('active')) {
            closeMenu();
        } else {
            openMenu();
        }
    });
    
    mobileOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
    
    // Close menu when a link is clicked
    navLinks.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => {
        link.addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 767) closeMenu();
        });
    });

    // ===== SCATTER & RESET ANIMATION =====
    function triggerScatter() {
        scatterElements.forEach(el => {
            // Random scatter values
            const sx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60;
            const sy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60;
            const sr = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30;
            el.style.setProperty('--sx', sx + 'px');
            el.style.setProperty('--sy', sy + 'px');
            el.style.setProperty('--sr', sr + 'deg');
            el.classList.add('scattering');
        });
        
        // Reset after a short delay
        setTimeout(() => {
            scatterElements.forEach(el => {
                el.classList.remove('scattering');
                el.classList.add('resetting');
            });
            setTimeout(() => {
                scatterElements.forEach(el => {
                    el.classList.remove('resetting');
                });
            }, 600);
        }, 400);
    }

    // Trigger scatter when user scrolls to features section for the first time
    let scattered = false;
    const featuresSection = document.getElementById('features');
    
    const scatterObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting && !scattered) {
                scattered = true;
                setTimeout(triggerScatter, 500);
                scatterObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
            }
        });
    }, { threshold: 0.3 });
    
    if (featuresSection) scatterObserver.observe(featuresSection);

    // ===== INTERSECTION OBSERVER FOR LAZY SECTIONS =====
    const sectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                entry.target.classList.add('revealed');
                sectionObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
            }
        });
    }, { threshold: 0.1 });
    
    lazySections.forEach(section => sectionObserver.observe(section));

    // ===== LAZY LOAD IMAGES =====
    const imageObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
        entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                const img = entry.target;
                if (img.dataset.src) {
                    img.src = img.dataset.src;
                    img.removeAttribute('data-src');
                    img.addEventListener('load', () => img.classList.add('loaded'));
                }
                imageObserver.unobserve(img);
            }
        });
    }, { rootMargin: '150px' });
    
    lazyImages.forEach(img => imageObserver.observe(img));

    // ===== SCROLL PROGRESS BAR =====
    const progressBar = document.createElement('div');
    progressBar.classList.add('scroll-progress');
    document.body.prepend(progressBar);
    
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        const scrollTop = window.scrollY;
        const docHeight = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        const scrolled = (scrollTop / docHeight) * 100;
        progressBar.style.width = scrolled + '%';
    });

    // ===== HEADER SCROLL EFFECT =====
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if (window.scrollY > 50) {
            header.style.boxShadow = '0 8px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
        } else {
            header.style.boxShadow = '0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
        }
    });

    // ===== SMOOTH SCROLL FOR ANCHOR LINKS =====
    document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(anchor => {
        anchor.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
            if (targetId === '#') return;
            const target = document.querySelector(targetId);
            if (target) {
                target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            }
        });
    });

    // ===== ESC KEY CLOSES MOBILE MENU =====
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && navLinks.classList.contains('active')) {
            closeMenu();
        }
    });

    console.log(' STREAK & DISCIPLINE â€” Advanced JS loaded');
})();

// ===== EXPERIENCE MODE TOGGLE =====
(function() {
    const toggle = document.getElementById('experienceToggle');
    const body = document.body;
    const rainContainer = document.getElementById('rainContainer');
    const emberContainer = document.getElementById('emberContainer');
    const STORAGE_KEY = 'sd-experience-mode';
    
    if (!toggle) return;
    
    // Check saved preference
    const savedMode = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (savedMode === 'active') {
        body.classList.add('experience-active');
        toggle.classList.add('active');
        startRain();
        startEmbers();
    }
    
    toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (body.classList.contains('experience-active')) {
            // TURN OFF
            body.classList.remove('experience-active');
            toggle.classList.remove('active');
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, 'inactive');
            stopRain();
            stopEmbers();
        } else {
            // TURN ON
            body.classList.add('experience-active');
            toggle.classList.add('active');
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, 'active');
            startRain();
            startEmbers();
        }
    });
    
    // ===== RAIN GENERATOR =====
    let rainInterval;
    const raindrops = [];
    const MAX_RAINDROPS = 60;
    
    function createRaindrop() {
        const drop = document.createElement('div');
        drop.className = 'raindrop';
        
        const left = Math.random() * 100;
        const duration = 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.8;
        const delay = Math.random() * 2;
        const size = 1 + Math.random() * 2;
        const opacity = 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.3;
        
        drop.style.cssText = `
            position: absolute;
            left: ${left}%;
            top: -40px;
            width: ${size}px;
            height: ${15 + Math.random() * 25}px;
            background: linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent, rgba(147, 197, 253, ${opacity}));
            border-radius: 0 0 3px 3px;
            animation: rainFall ${duration}s linear ${delay}s infinite;
            pointer-events: none;
        `;
        
        return drop;
    }
    
    function startRain() {
        if (!rainContainer) return;
        rainContainer.innerHTML = '';
        
        // Add CSS keyframe if not exists
        if (!document.getElementById('rain-keyframes')) {
            const style = document.createElement('style');
            style.id = 'rain-keyframes';
            style.textContent = `
                @keyframes rainFall {
                    0%   { transform: translateY(-40px) translateX(0); opacity: 0; }
                    10%  { opacity: 1; }
                    90%  { opacity: 1; }
                    100% { transform: translateY(100vh) translateX(${20 + Math.random() * 30}px); opacity: 0; }
                }
            `;
            document.head.appendChild(style);
        }
        
        for (let i = 0; i < MAX_RAINDROPS; i++) {
            const drop = createRaindrop();
            rainContainer.appendChild(drop);
            raindrops.push(drop);
        }
    }
    
    function stopRain() {
        if (!rainContainer) return;
        rainContainer.innerHTML = '';
        raindrops.length = 0;
    }
    
    // ===== EMBER / SPARK GENERATOR =====
    let emberInterval;
    const embers = [];
    const MAX_EMBERS = 25;
    
    function createEmber() {
        const ember = document.createElement('div');
        ember.className = 'ember';
        
        const left = Math.random() * 100;
        const size = 2 + Math.random() * 4;
        const duration = 3 + Math.random() * 5;
        const delay = Math.random() * 4;
        const color = Math.random() > 0.5 ? '147, 197, 253' : '96, 165, 250'; // Blue tones
        
        ember.style.cssText = `
            position: absolute;
            left: ${left}%;
            bottom: -10px;
            width: ${size}px;
            height: ${size}px;
            background: rgba(${color}, 0.6);
            border-radius: 50%;
            box-shadow: 0 0 ${size * 2}px rgba(${color}, 0.4);
            animation: emberRise ${duration}s linear ${delay}s infinite;
            pointer-events: none;
        `;
        
        return ember;
    }
    
    function startEmbers() {
        if (!emberContainer) return;
        emberContainer.innerHTML = '';
        
        if (!document.getElementById('ember-keyframes')) {
            const style = document.createElement('style');
            style.id = 'ember-keyframes';
            style.textContent = `
                @keyframes emberRise {
                    0%   { transform: translateY(0) translateX(0) scale(1); opacity: 0.8; }
                    50%  { transform: translateY(-50vh) translateX(${10 + Math.random() * 40}px) scale(0.6); opacity: 0.4; }
                    100% { transform: translateY(-100vh) translateX(${-10 - Math.random() * 30}px) scale(0); opacity: 0; }
                }
            `;
            document.head.appendChild(style);
        }
        
        for (let i = 0; i < MAX_EMBERS; i++) {
            const ember = createEmber();
            emberContainer.appendChild(ember);
            embers.push(ember);
        }
    }
    
    function stopEmbers() {
        if (!emberContainer) return;
        emberContainer.innerHTML = '';
        embers.length = 0;
    }
    
})();


onboarding_advance.css

/* ============================================================
   ONBOARDING ADVANCE CSS  —  STREAK & DISCIPLINE
   Deferred / non-blocking. Loaded after light CSS.
   All animations, spring physics, 3D, glow, glassmorphism.
   "GTA-level physics" — mass, overshoot, settle.
   ============================================================ */

/* ---- Spring easing catalogue ----------------------------- */
/* Overshoot (door swings open)     */ /* cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1) */
/* Fast decelerate (car braking)    */ /* cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)    */
/* Elastic snap (rubber band)       */ /* cubic-bezier(0.68,-0.55,0.265,1.55) */
/* Heavy settle (dropped object)    */ /* cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)     */

/* ============================================================
   PHASE ENTRANCE / EXIT ANIMATIONS
   ============================================================ */

/* Card entrance — slides up, overshoots, settles */
@keyframes phaseIn {
  0%   { opacity:0; transform: translateY(48px) scale(0.94); }
  55%  { opacity:1; transform: translateY(-7px) scale(1.008); }
  75%  { transform: translateY(3px) scale(0.997); }
  100% { transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
}

/* Full-screen phase entrance */

  0%   { opacity:0; transform: translateY(60px); }
  60%  { opacity:1; transform: translateY(-8px); }
  80%  { transform: translateY(4px); }
  100% { transform: translateY(0); }
}

/* Card exit — fly off upward */

  0%   { opacity:1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
  100% { opacity:0; transform: translateY(-42px) scale(0.95); }
}

<!--Start of Tawk.to Script-->
<script type="text/javascript">
var Tawk_API=Tawk_API||{}, Tawk_LoadStart=new Date();
(function(){
var s1=document.createElement("script"),s0=document.getElementsByTagName("script")[0];
s1.async=true;
s1.src='https://embed.tawk.to/6a5e73d9aa83a11d48ca5748/1ju0f92jn';
s1.charset='UTF-8';
s1.setAttribute('crossorigin','*');
s0.parentNode.insertBefore(s1,s0);
})();
</script>
<!--End of Tawk.to Script-->

demo_weekly_analysis.css(the light version of cdn/demo_weekly_analysis.css)
 /* ============================================================
   demo_weekly_analysis.css
   LIGHT / CRITICAL-PATH STYLESHEET
   Loaded synchronously so the report is usable and legible the
   instant HTML parses. Contains ONLY what is needed to avoid a
   flash of unstyled content: tokens, reset, nav, cookie banner,
   loader, and a bare-bones layout skeleton. All "wow" visuals
   (paint textures, 3D tilt, spring physics, chart animation)
   live in /cdn/demo_weekly_analysis.css and are swapped in once
   that file finishes downloading. Nothing in here should be
   considered final polish — it just has to not look broken.
   ============================================================ */

config.py
from django.core.cache import cache
import random
from django.utils import timezone
from django.templatetags.static import static
import os


def get_consistency_Value() -> str:
    """Return a string to be set in the consistency text on the landing_page, it send that same string to cache and it remain active for two minutes before it get reset again """
    consistency = cache.get('consistency')
    if consistency is None:
        consistency = random.randint(94,98)
        cache.set('consistency', consistency, timeout=60*2)
        
    return str(consistency)

def get_journal_created_value()->str:
    """set a primary key to the cache on first page visit, on each page visit, get the key, increment it by one and store it back again
    NB: THIS IS JUST A TEMPRORAY SOLUTION"""
    journal_created = cache.get('journal_created')
    if journal_created is None:
        journal_created = 10
    else:journal_created += 0.2
    
    cache.set('journal_created', journal_created)
    
    return str(round(journal_created))

def get_copyright_year():
    return (timezone.now().year)

class Static:
    
    def __int__(self):
        """For data that will not change and are short """
        pass
    @classmethod
    def token_lenght(self) -> int:
        """Lenght for token in url"""
        return 8
    @classmethod
    def logo_url(self) -> str:
        """Logo url for the site"""
        return 'https://res.cloudinary.com/brop3jeq/image/upload/v1784524604/logo_jvljxp.png'
    
    @classmethod
    def token_expiry_time(self)-> int:
        """The Official amount of seconds it takes before expirey"""
        return int(10*60)
    @classmethod
    def custom_base_url(self) -> str:
        """the official domain of streak adn discipline"""
        return os.getenv('CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS', 'http://localhost:8002').split(',')[0]# incase i need to access base url in templates in situations where i cannot use {% url ''%}
    @classmethod
    def official_email(self) -> str:
        """The official email account i use to send email to users"""
        return "STREAK & DISCIPLINE <noreply@resend.dev>"
    
    


email_sending.py

import os, resend, random
from utility.config import Static
from time import time

def send_password_reset_email(to_email: str, endpoint: str, expiry: int, username: str) -> None:
    """Sends email with link for password reset."""
    
    api_key = os.getenv("RESEND_API_KEY")
    resend.api_key = api_key
    resend.Emails.send({
        "from": Static.official_email(),
        "to": [to_email],
        "subject": "Password Reset Link — STREAK & DISCIPLINE",
        "html": """
        <div style="max-width:500px;margin:0 auto;font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,sans-serif;background:#fff;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid #e2e8f0">
            <table style="width:100%;padding:24px">
                <tr>
                    <td style="width:48px;vertical-align:top;padding-right:16px">
                        <img src="{logo_url}" alt="S&D" style="width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:8px">
                    </td>
                    <td style="vertical-align:top">
                        <p style="margin:0 0 2px;font-weight:700;font-size:16px;color:#0f172a">STREAK & DISCIPLINE</p>
                        <p style="margin:0;font-size:13px;color:#64748b">Password Reset</p>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
            <div style="padding:0 24px 20px">
                <p style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:14px;color:#334155;line-height:1.6">Hello <strong>{username}</strong>, we received a request to reset the password for your account.</p>
                <a href="{link}" style="display:inline-block;padding:12px 28px;background:#2563eb;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:16px">Reset My Password</a>
                <p style="margin:0 0 10px;font-size:12px;color:#64748b;line-height:1.5">This link takes you to a secure page where you can choose a new password. It expires in <strong>{expiry} minutes</strong> and can only be used once. scrunity: {identifier_to_make_each_email_unique}</p>
                <div style="background:#fff7ed;border:1px solid #fed7aa;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:6px;margin-bottom:14px">
                    <p style="margin:0;font-size:11px;color:#9a3412;line-height:1.5"><strong>&#9888; Don't share this link.</strong> Nobody from STREAK & DISCIPLINE will ever ask for it. If you didn't request this, ignore this email — your account is safe.</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:#f8fafc;padding:12px 24px;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0;text-align:center">
                <p style="margin:0;font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8">Need help? <a href="mailto:issaabdulsalamope11@gmail.com" style="color:#2563eb">support@streakanddiscipline.com</a></p>
            </div>
        </div>
        """.format(link=Static.custom_base_url() + endpoint, expiry=int(expiry/60), username=username, logo_url=Static.logo_url(), identifier_to_make_each_email_unique = "".join(random.sample("{username}{link}{time}", 8)))})
